## Verdict (Gate-Bubbles-3, 12 Nov 2026): GO

Re-gate of PR #831 (POLICY_CITATION flip fixes) at 2c4204de, after my NO-GO in Rpcf. Merged --no-ff onto main 7e8d04d8 (gate merge 433532a7). The merge was clean and tsc is clean. 1 OFF + 1 ON. OFF = production on + assumedOn; ON = + POLICY_CITATION_ENABLED. 14,831 tests.

838 changed: **INTENDED 4, INTENDED-HARNESS 1, INTENDED-ADJACENT 833, REGRESSION 0, FLAKY 0.**
- I diffed every replay row OFF vs ON. In each INTENDED-ADJACENT row, the only change is the inserted "**Policy cited from the JBA Policy Manual**" block. Text, updates and ok are otherwise byte-identical.
- Two exceptions, both already explained in Rpcf: nov6-policy-change-relevant-remove-attendance / nov7-a1-remove-policy-created ("already cites … Nothing needed changing"), and nov3-stepb-* ("The Action Plan now states the payroll deduction.").
- The INTENDED-HARNESS row is flags-off-parity, the replay pack's second half. Its child run inherits the flag, so the 2 nov8-560 open cases hold there and fail their open pins.

**Rpcf's regressions, checked by name:**
1. **Set-aside** (nov8-policy-set-aside-ca-card, -ca-create, -coaching-card, -coaching-create, -named-again, -stays-off, -unnamed, nov8-set-aside-plan, -plan-other-policy, nov7-a1-remove-policy-card; both modes): **fixed.**
   - No reply after "take the … policy off" carries the block.
